{"etymology_number":"1","forms":[{"form":"nests","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"glosses":["A structure built by a bird as a place to incubate eggs and rear young."]},{"examples":[{"text":"mouse nests, wasp nests, skink nests","type":"example"},{"text":"Trapdoor spider nests are vertical tubes in the ground, lined with silk.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Any of various similar structures or sites used by other animals:","A structure built, or site modified, to incubate eggs, rear young, or shelter a colony."],"topics":["biology","natural-sciences","zoology"]},{"examples":[{"text":"Chimpanzees bend and weave fresh tree branches to create a comfortable nest for the night.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Any of various similar structures or sites used by other animals:","A temporary structure or modified site created by an animal for sleeping, resting, or protection from the elements."],"topics":["biology","natural-sciences","zoology"]},{"examples":[{"text":"The male stickleback glues together pieces of algae to form a tunnel-like nest to attract a mate.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Any of various similar structures or sites used by other animals:","A depression, mound, or localized structure prepared by an aquatic animal for spawning and guarding eggs."],"topics":["biology","natural-sciences","zoology"]},{"glosses":["A snug, comfortable, or cosy residence or job situation."]},{"glosses":["A retreat, or place of habitual resort."]},{"examples":[{"text":"a nest of thieves","type":"example"},{"text":"That nightclub is a nest of strange people!","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A hideout for bad people to frequent or haunt; a den."]},{"examples":[{"text":"I am aspiring to leave the nest.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A home that a child or young adult shares with a parent or guardian."]},{"examples":[{"text":"I was forced to change trumps when I found the ace, jack, and nine of diamonds in the nest.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A fixed number of cards in some bidding games awarded to the highest bidder allowing him to exchange any or all with cards in his hand."],"topics":["card-games","games"]},{"examples":[{"text":"a machine gun nest","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A fortified position for a weapon."],"topics":["government","military","politics","war"]},{"glosses":["A structure consisting of nested structures, such as nested loops or nested subroutine calls."],"topics":["computing","engineering","mathematics","natural-sciences","physical-sciences","sciences"]},{"glosses":["A circular bed of pasta, rice, etc. to be topped or filled with other foods."]},{"glosses":["An aggregated mass of any ore or mineral, in an isolated state, within a rock."],"topics":["geography","geology","natural-sciences"]},{"glosses":["A collection of boxes, cases, or the like, of graduated size, each put within the one next larger."]},{"glosses":["A compact group of pulleys, gears, springs, etc., working together or collectively."]},{"glosses":["The pubic hair near a vulva or a vulva itself."],"synonyms":[{"word":"beav"},{"word":"beaver"}],"tags":["US","slang","vulgar"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/nɛst/"}],"upstream_line":207058,"upstream_offset":998215435,"upstream_record_sha256":"4d49a45e185f44aebf56049bf927c2ff1ab986831dd4d1b5b15987ebd67c1cfe","word":"nest"}
{"etymology_number":"2","forms":[{"form":"nests","tags":["present","singular","third-person"]},{"form":"nesting","tags":["participle","present"]},{"form":"nested","tags":["participle","past"]},{"form":"nested","tags":["past"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"verb","senses":[{"glosses":["To build or settle into a nest."],"raw_tags":["of animals"],"tags":["intransitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"We loved the new house and were nesting there in two days!","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To settle into a home."],"tags":["intransitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"I bought a set of nesting mixing bowls for my mother.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To successively neatly fit inside another."],"tags":["intransitive"]},{"glosses":["To place in, or as if in, a nest."],"tags":["transitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"There would be much more room in the attic if you had nested all the empty boxes.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To place one thing neatly inside another, and both inside yet another (and so on)."],"tags":["transitive"]},{"glosses":["To hunt for birds' nests or their contents (usually \"go nesting\")."],"tags":["intransitive"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/nɛst/"}],"upstream_line":207059,"upstream_offset":998297725,"upstream_record_sha256":"b4ad7a44518d3fe9912c8c72f9f5a01579a8f5dcdb639b3f867356379c376dcf","word":"nest"}
